Chairman’s Statement
Chairman’s
董事長致辭


Statement
董事長致辭
To the Shareholders,
On behalf of the board (the “Board”) of directors (the
“Directors”) of Nanjing Sinolife United Company Limited*
(the “Company”, together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”),
I am pleased to present the annual results of the Group for
the year ended 31 December 2023 (the “Year”).


致股東
本人代表南京中生聯合股份有限公司（「本公司」，連同
其附屬公司統稱「本集團」）董事（「董事」）會（「董事會」）
欣 然 提 呈 本 集 團 截 至 2023 年 12 月 31 日 止 年 度（「本 年
度」）的全年業績。

* For identification purpose only
* 僅供識別

Management Discussion and Analysis
管理層討論及分析

BUSINESS REVIEW                                                                     業務回顧
With clear strategic objectives and determined implementation by the                得益於清晰的戰略目標以及管理層對實現戰
management, the Group achieved remarkable operational and financial                 略目標的堅決執行，本集團在 2023 年取得了
performance in 2023. The Group witnessed rapid development in both key              不錯的經營和財務業績。本集團兩大核心業
business segments, namely the cross-border e-commerce platforms and the             務板塊，跨境電子商務平臺和嬰童類經銷商
distribution channels for infant and child series products. The Group’s revenue     銷 售，均 取 得 快 速 發 展。本 集 團 於 本 年 度 的
for the Year amounted to approximately RMB564.0 million, representing an            收益約為人民幣 564.0 百萬元，較 2022 年的約
increase of approximately 105.2% compared to approximately RMB274.9                 人 民 幣 274.9 百 萬 元 增 加 約 105.2%。本 集 團 於
million in 2022. The Group recorded a profit of approximately RMB52.6 million       本 年 度 錄 得 利 潤 約 人 民 幣 52.6 百 萬 元，而 於
for the Year, turning loss into profit as compared to the loss of approximately     2022 年 則 錄 得 虧 損 約 人 民 幣 14.2 百 萬 元，實
14.2 million RMB recorded in 2022.                                                  現扭虧為盈。

In terms of supply chain management, the Group has also achieved the goals          供 應 鏈 管 理，也 完 成 年 初 既 定 目 標。於 本 年
set at the beginning of the year. During the Year, the Group boosted its            度內，本集團通過加強供應鏈系統的管理，
production capacity by strengthening the management of its supply chain             發 展 優 質 供 貨 商，提 升 產 能，以 滿 足 快 速 增
system and identifying high-quality suppliers, so as to accommodate the fast-       長的跨境電子商務平臺以及嬰童類產品市場
growing demands from the cross-border e-commerce platform and the infant            的需求；存貨周轉效率也得以提升。
and child series products in the market. As a result, the efficiency of inventory
turnover has also improved.

BOARD COMMITTEES                                                                  董事委員會
The Board delegates certain responsibilities to various committees. In            董事會將部分職責委派給多個委員會。根據
accordance with the relevant PRC laws and regulations and the corporate           中國相關法律及法規以及香港聯合交易所有
governance practice prescribed in the Rules (the “Listing Rules”) Governing       限公司（「聯交所」）證券上市規則（「上市規則」）
the Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the         訂明的企業管治常規，本公司已設立四個董
“Stock Exchange”), the Company has formed four Board committees, namely           事 會 委 員 會，即 審 核 委 員 會、薪 酬 委 員 會、
the Audit Committee, the Remuneration Committee, the Nomination                   提名委員會及戰略及發展委員會。
Committee, and the Strategy and Development Committee.

Audit Committee                                                                   審核委員會
The Board established an Audit Committee with written terms of reference.         董事會成立審核委員會，並制定書面職權範
The primary duties of the Audit Committee are to assist the Board in reviewing    圍。審核委員會的主要職責為協助董事會審
the financial information and reporting process, risk management and internal     閱財務資料及申報程序、風險管理及內部監
control systems, effectiveness of internal audit function, scope of audit and     控 制 度、內 部 審 核 職 能 的 有 效 性、外 聘 核 數

企業管治報告

The Board is pleased to report to the Shareholders on the corporate                  董事會欣然向股東呈報本公司截至 2023 年 12
governance of the Company for the year ended 31 December 2023.                       月 31 日止年度的企業管治報告。

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE CULTURE AND VALUE                                               企業管治文化及價值
The Company is committed to ensuring that its affairs are conducted in               本公司致力確保以崇高的商業道德標準營運
accordance with high business ethical standards. This reflects its belief that, in   業務，反映其堅信如要達到長遠的業務目標，
the achievement of its long-term business objectives, it is imperative to act        必 須 以 誠 信、透 明 和 負 責 的 態 度 行 事。本 公
with probity, transparency and accountability. By doing so, the Company              司相信這樣長遠可為股東取得最大的回報，
believes that Shareholders’ return will be maximised in the long term and that       而僱員、業務夥伴及公司營運業務的社區亦
its employees, those with whom it has business activity and the communities          可受惠。
in which it operates will all benefit.

Corporate governance is the process by which the Board instructs                     企業管治是董事局指導集團管理層如何營運
management of the Group to conduct its affairs with a view to ensuring that its      業務以達成業務目標的過程。董事局致力維
objectives are met. The Board is committed to maintaining and developing             持及建立完善的企業管治常規，以確保：
robust corporate governance practices that are intended to ensure:

•     satisfactory and sustainable returns to Shareholders;                          •   為股東帶來滿意及可持續的回報；

•     that the interests of those who deal with the Company are safeguarded;         •   保障本公司業務夥伴的利益；

      customers; and                                                                     及

•     that high standards of business ethics are maintained.                         •   維持崇高的商業道德標準。

Board Diversity Policy                                                             董事會多元化政策
The Company has adopted a Board Diversity Policy which sets out the                本公司已採納董事會多元化政策，當中載列
approach to achieve diversity of the Board.                                        實現董事會多元化的方式。

The Company recognizes and embraces the benefits of having a diverse Board         本公司了解及認同具有多元化董事會成員的
to enhance the quality of its performance. A truly diverse Board will include      禆 益，以 提 升 其 表 現 質 素。一 個 真 正 多 元 化
and make good use of differences in the skills, regional and industry              的董事會將包括具備不同技能、地區及行業
experience, background, race, gender and other qualities of Directors. These       經 驗、背 景、種 族、性 別 及 其 他 特 質 的 董 事
differences will be taken into account in determining the optimum                  會 成 員，並 可 加 以 利 用。本 公 司 在 制 定 董 事
composition of the Board. All Board appointments will be based on merit while      會成員的最佳組合時將考慮上述不同之處。
taking into account diversity (including gender diversity). The Company aims       所有董事會成員之任命均以用人唯才為原則
to build and maintain a Board with a diversity of Directors, in terms of skills,   並考慮多元化（包括性別多元化）。本公司旨
experience, knowledge, expertise, culture, independence, age and gender.           在技能、經驗、知識、專長、文化、獨立性、
                                                                                   年齡及性別方面建立及保持董事會多元化的
                                                                                   董事成員。

The Company aims to maintain an appropriate balance of diversity                   本公司致力在與本公司業務增長相關的多元
perspectives that are relevant to the Company’s business growth and is also        化層面維持適度均衡，同時盡力確保由董事
committed to ensuring that recruitment and selection practices at all levels       會自上而下各層面的招聘及挑選常規有合適
(from the Board downwards) are appropriately structured so that a diverse          架構，致使能夠考慮多元化的人選。
range of candidates are considered.

diversity with reference to the stakeholders’ expectation and international and    和本地推薦的最佳實踐，保持性別多元化的

The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk management and                     董事會承認其對本公司及其附屬公司的風險
internal control systems of the Company and its subsidiaries and reviewing                管理及內部監控制度以及每年審核其有效性
their effectiveness annually. Such systems are designed to manage rather than             的責任。該等制度旨在管理而非消除未能達
eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives and can only provide         成業務目標的風險，而且只能就不會有重大
reasonable and not absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss.              的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對的保證。

The Board has the responsibility for establishing, maintaining and evaluating             董事會負責制定、維持及評估有效的風險管
effective risk management and internal control systems. The board of                      理及內部監控制度。監事會負責監督董事會

implementation of risk management and internal control systems. The                       層負責協助及監察風險管理及內部監控過程。
management is responsible for coordinating and monitoring the risk                        審核委員會協助董事會有關內部監控的領導
management and internal control progress. The Audit Committee assists the                 及 協 作 事 宜、監 督 設 計、實 施 及 監 察 風 險 管
Board in leading and coordinating matters regarding internal control,                     理及內部監控制度。
overseeing the design, implementation and monitoring of the risk

COMPLIANCE FIRST, SHARED RESPONSIBILITIES                                              合規先行，讓權責共履
The Group recognizes the significance of sound corporate governance as the             本集團深刻理解良好的公司治理是企業發展
cornerstone and guarantee for corporate development. We integrate the                  的基石和保障。我們將可持續發展理念融入
concept of sustainable development into our business operations,                       企 業 營 運 中，持 續 優 化 企 業 管 治、強 化 風 險
continuously optimize corporate governance, strengthen risk control, and               管 控、回 應 多 元 利 益 相 關 方 期 望，以 此 創 造
respond to the expectations of diverse stakeholders, so as to create a compliant       合規穩健的經營環境，提升集團的長期競爭
and stable operating environment, enhance the Group’s long-term competitiveness        力和經營韌性，促進本集團與各利益相關方
and business resilience, and promote sustainable development with all stakeholders.    的可持續發展。

ESG Governance                                                                         ESG 管治
The Group consistently upholds the principles of sustainable development               本集團始終踐行可持續發展理念，在保障集
while ensuring stable business operations, giving due consideration to ESG             團穩定經營的同時充分考慮 ESG 管理。我們不
management. We continuously enhance our ESG management structure,                      斷優化 ESG 管理架構，提升自身 ESG 管理水平，
elevate our own ESG management standards, listen to and respond to                     傾聽並回應各利益相關方對本集團 ESG 方面的
suggestions and expectations regarding the Group’s ESG matters from various            建議去期望，推動價值鏈可持續發展。
stakeholders, and drive sustainable development across the value chain.

Governance Structure                                                                   管治架構
The Group is committed to steadily enhancing the Company’s sustainable                 本集團致力於通過全面、自上而下的管理與
development capability through comprehensive, top-down management and                  協 作，穩 步 提 升 公 司 可 持 續 發 展 能 力，積 極
collaboration, and actively promoting ESG work. We have established a top-             推 進 ESG 工 作。我 們 建 立 了 由 董 事 會、ESG 管
down three-tier ESG governance structure consisting of the Board, the ESG              理委員會和 ESG 工作小組組成的自上而下的三
Management Committee and the ESG Task Force, and clarified the powers and              級 ESG 管 治 架 構。各 層 級 權 責 分 明，通 過 規
responsibilities at all levels. By adopting standardized, systematic, and scientific   範、系統和科學的管理方式，推動本集團 ESG
management methods, the Group actively promotes the implementation of                  相關決策和工作的實施，通過實際行動履行
the Group’s ESG-related decisions and work, and fulfils its ESG commitments            ESG 承諾。
through practical actions.

Board Statement                                                                     董事會聲明
Overall Responsibility Borne by the Board                                           董事會肩負總體責任
As the highest decision-making body for ESG matters, the Board assumes              董事會作為 ESG 工作最高決策機構，對本集團
overall supervision, guidance and review responsibilities for the Group’s ESG       ESG 工作承擔整體監察、指導及檢討責任。董
initiatives. The responsibilities of the Board include establishment of ESG         事會肩負制定集團的 ESG 策略方針、審定目標

and identification, ESG performance monitoring, and approval of the annual          表現，以及審批年度 ESG 報告等職責。董事會
ESG report. The Board authorizes the ESG Management Committee to assist in          授權 ESG 管理委員會協助監督 ESG 策略實施，
monitoring the implementation of the ESG strategy, fully supporting the             全力推動本集團可持續發展進程。
Group’s sustainable development efforts.

development, an internal and external analysis and the Guide.                                                          況以及《指引》，回顧、識別出 2023 年 ESG 議題
                                                                                                                       清單。

conducted comprehensive analysis and assessment of relevant issues and                                                 合分析與評估，得出 2023 年重大性議題矩陣，

development management.

 Committed to zero-additive, safeguarding the health of users
 致力零添加，守護用戶健康
 In 2023, the Group adhered to the principle of avoiding the use of additives as much as possible and minimizing the use of
 additives when necessary for all newly developed products. Additives are only used when necessary based on process technology
 and have been proven safe and reliable after risk assessment, and their usage in products is minimized as much as possible.
 2023 年，本集團所有新開發的產品均秉持添加劑能不用儘量不用，必須用儘量少用的原則，只有在工藝技術上確有
 必要且經過風險評估證明安全可靠的情況下使用添加劑，且儘可能降低其在產品中的使用量。

 During the Reporting Period, we have developed new products such as Colostrum Powder（牛 初 乳 粉）and HMO (Human Milk
 Oligosaccharides) Probiotic Powder (HMO（母乳低聚糖）益生菌粉）without the use any food additives, and all ingredients used
 were healthy materials that are beneficial for the human body.
 報告期內，新開發的牛初乳粉、HMO（母乳低聚糖）益生菌粉等產品中未使用食品添加劑，所有配料均為對身體健康
 有益的健康原料。

 Preferred Healthy and Safe Ingredients
 優選健康、安全原料
 HMO plays an irreplaceable role in promoting the growth of infant intestinal flora, improving the function of the immune system,
 and supporting brain development. Although the use of new HMO ingredients was approved in China in 2023, after thorough
 evaluation based on factors such as quality stability and clinical data support, we decided to select a safer internationally renowned
 brand of HMO material.
 HMO 在促進嬰兒腸道菌群生長、完善免疫系統功能，以及促進大腦發育等方面發揮著不可替代的作用。2023 年，
 HMO 新原料在中國批准使用，但我們經過深入評估，基於品質穩定性、臨床數據支持等綜合分析，我們仍決定選用
 更為安全的國際知名品牌 HMO 原料。

 Development of New Product by Living Nature: Aqua Gel Mask
 Living Nature 開發新產品 Aqua Gel Mask（水凝膠面膜）
 During the Reporting Period, Living Nature successfully developed a new product, namely Aqua Gel Mask. The tube material used
 in this product is made from 30% PCR (post-recycled plastic), and its outer packaging is made from soy-based ink, which is 100%
 recyclable and free from toxins or chlorine bleach. The outer packaging material has obtained PEFC (Programme for the
 Endorsement of Forest Certification) certification, which is a globally recognized forest certification system.
 報告期間，Living Nature 成功開發新產品 Aqua Gel Mask（水凝膠面膜）。其使用的軟管材料 30% 來源於 PCR（post recycled
 plastic，消費後回收塑料），且其外包裝由大豆油墨製成，可 100% 循環利用，不含毒素或氯元素漂白劑。該外包裝材
 料已獲得 PEFC（Programme for the Endorsement of Forest Certification，泛歐森林認證，全球領先的國家森林認證體系聯盟）
 認證。

 employee motivation but also significantly promoted the development of the Douyin and Kuaishou projects.

 E-commerce Training Specialized in The First Window of Store
 The First Window of Store 電商專題培訓
 During the Reporting Period, we conducted the e-commerce training specialized in “The First Window of Store”. This training aimed
 to enhance the e-commerce business know-how and skills of our employees, thereby strengthening the overall business
 capabilities of the team and improving our business efficiency.
 報告期內，我們開展了「The First Window of Store」電商專題培訓。此次培訓旨在通過提升員工的電商業務知識和技能，
 進而增強團隊整體業務能力，以實現業務效率的提升。

 During the training process, the e-commerce director conducted an objective review of the previous quarter’s work, whereby
 analyzing the achievement of various indicators in detail, and scientifically breaking down the targets for the next quarter.
 Simultaneously, a comprehensive review and in-depth discussion were conducted regarding the current business situation and
 existing issues within the team, with practical future development directions and improvement recommendations proposed. This
 training session received a positive response from the employees, effectively enhancing their business capabilities and professional
 qualifications. It further improved the match between employees and their roles, boosting our business efficiency.
 在培訓過程中，電商總監對上季度工作進行了客觀的複盤，詳細分析了各項指標的達成情況，並對下季度目標進行
 了科學分解。同時，針對團隊當前的業務現狀和存在的問題，進行了全面的梳理和深入的探討，提出了切實可行的
 未來發展方向和改進建議。此次培訓活動得到了員工的積極響應，有效提升了員工的業務能力和專業素養，使人崗
 匹配度得到進一步提升，推動業務效率的提升。

 Proactively Conducting Safety Inspection and Risk Prevention and Investigation
 積極開展安全風險防控排查
 The Group actively engages in risk control activities under the working notion of “safety first, prevention as priority” to preemptively
 identify potential risks threatening business operations. In our daily operations, we actively carry out risk inspections. The
 engineering department of the Group rigorously checks firefighting equipment in accordance with regulations and ensures
 responsibilities are assigned to individuals, fully safeguarding firefighting safety. The Group places a strong emphasis on electrical
 safety, where we have staff on 24-hour duty in the central control room for monitoring. Furthermore, we arrange for personnel to
 patrol at night to investigate and rectify electrical safety hazards.
 本集團遵循「安全第一，預防為主」的工作思路，積極開展風險防控工作，對企業營運可能遭受的風險提前進行識別。
 在企業日常營運過程中，我們積極開展風險排查工作，集團工程部嚴格按照規定檢查消防器材，並將責任落實到人，
 全力守護消防安全。本集團大力強調用電安全問題，我們在中控室安排人員 24 小時值守並進行監測，我們亦安排相
 關人員在夜間進行巡邏，排查用電安全隱患。




                                              Regular Firefighting Equipment Inspection

 Safety Training
 安全培訓
 To ensure employees’ safety and health during the production process, and prevent and reduce accidents, we regularly organize
 and conduct safety training. During the Reporting Period, we organized production safety training sessions, including electrician
 duty training, hot work safety trainings, and warehouse safety training. Such training aims to effectively enhance employees’
 awareness of safety prevention, improve emergency response and professional skills, and ensure employees’ health and safety
 comprehensively through system requirements, emergency handling processes and measures, case sharing, and practice.
 為確保員工在生產過程中的安全和健康，防止和減少事故的發生，我們定期組織開展安全培訓。報告期內，我們組
 織開展電工職責培訓、動火作業安全培訓、倉庫安全培訓等生產安全培訓。培訓旨在通過制度要求、應急處置流程
 與措施、案例分享及實踐，有效提升員工安全預防意識、突發情況應對和專業技能，全方位確保員工的健康與安全。



At the same time, the Group actively organizes fire drills and participates in fire   同 時，本 集 團 積 極 組 織 開 展 消 防 演 習，積 極
drill activities organized by the subdistrict offices to ensure our emergency         參加街道組織的消防演練活動，確保不斷提
response capabilities will continue to improve. Living Nature organizes a fire        升我們的應急能力。Living Nature 每六個月組
drill for all employees every six months.                                             織所有員工開展消防演習。

 Emergency Drills
 應急演練
 To enhance the timely, skilled and standardized rescue capabilities of maintenance personnel and improve the response to
 emergencies, we organized a series of activities during the Reporting Period, including emergency drills for elevator entrapment
 and injury from falling objects. These activities have promoted emergency knowledge, raise public awareness of risk prevention,
 and enhance disaster response capabilities such as self-rescue and mutual aid.
 為強化維保人員救援處置能力的及時性、熟練性和規範性，提升突發事件的應對能力。報告期內，我們組織開展電
 梯困人應急演練、物體打擊傷害應急演練等一系列活動，普及應急知識，提高公眾風險防範意識和自救互救等災害
 應對能力。

 Through the elevator entrapment emergency drills, we verified the effectiveness of the standard rescue procedures for elevator
 entrapment and the emergency response capabilities of our personnel, ensuring timely and effective responses in case of
 accidents. By conducting emergency drills for injury from falling objects, we comprehensively reviewed the issues in the
 contingency plan, thereby improving the emergency plan to enhance its practicality and operability. At the same time, these drills
 increase the familiarity of the organizing units, participating units, and personnel with the emergency plan, improving their
 emergency response capabilities.
 通過電梯困人應急演練，驗證電梯困人常規拯救程序的有效性和人員的應急能力，確保險情發生時的及時、有效應
 對。通過開展物體打擊傷害應急演練，我們全面排場預案中存在的問題，進而完善應急預案，提高應急預案的實用
 性和可操行，同時增強演練組織單位、參與單位和人員等對應急預案的熟悉程度，提高其應急處置能力。

OPINION                                                                           意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Nanjing Sinolife         我們已審計第 136 至 230 頁所載南京中生聯合
United Company Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (the “Group”)         股份有限公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（「貴
set out on pages 136 to 230, which comprise the consolidated statement of         集團」）之綜合財務報表，包括於 2023 年 12 月
financial position as at 31 December 2023, and the consolidated statement of      31 日之綜合財務狀況表及截至該日止年度之
profit or loss and other comprehensive income, the consolidated statement of      綜合損益及其他全面收益表、綜合權益變動
changes in equity and the consolidated statement of cash flows for the year       表及綜合現金流量表以及綜合財務報表附註，
then ended, and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including         包括重大會計政策資料。
material accounting policy information.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and fair view   我們認為，綜合財務報表根據香港會計師公
of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31 December 2023,       會（「香港會計師公會」）頒佈之香港財務報告
and of its consolidated financial performance and its consolidated cash flows     準則（「香港財務報告準則」）真實及公平地反
for the year then ended in accordance with Hong Kong Financial Reporting          映了貴集團於 2023 年 12 月 31 日之綜合財務狀
Standards (“HKFRSs”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public        況及截至該日止年度之綜合財務表現及其綜
Accountants (“HKICPA”) and have been properly prepared in compliance with         合現金流量，並已根據香港公司條例之披露
the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.                 規定妥善編製。

BASIS FOR OPINION                                                                 意見之基準
We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing         我們根據香港會計師公會頒佈之香港審計準
(“HKSAs”) issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities under those standards are    則（「香港審計準則」）進行審計工作。我們於
further described in the Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of the          該等準則項下之責任在本報告中核數師審計
consolidated financial statements section of our report. We are independent of    綜合財務報表之責任 一節進一步描述。根據
the Group in accordance with the HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional         香港會計師公會之專業會計師道德守則（「守
Accountants (the “Code”), and we have fulfilled our other ethical                 則」），我 們 獨 立 於 貴 集 團，而 我 們 已 根 據 守
responsibilities in accordance with the Code. We believe that the audit           則 履 行 其 他 道 德 責 任。我 們 認 為，我 們 獲 得
evidence we have obtained is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for    之審計憑證充足及適當，以為我們提供意見
our opinion.                                                                      基準。

thereon, and we do not provide a separate opinion on these matters. For each               不就該等事項提出單獨意見。就以下各項事
matter below, our description of how our audit addressed the matter is                     項而言，我們於文中描述我們之審計如何處
provided in that context.                                                                  理該事項。

performance of procedures designed to respond to our assessment of the risks               我們對綜合財務報表重大錯誤陳述風險之評

 根據香港會計準則第 36 號「資產減值」，貴集團須每年及於有跡象顯                                                         我們評估貴集團對商譽的減值評估的流程及
 示獲分配商譽的有關資產的現金產生單位（「現金產生單位」）可能減                                                           主要控制。我們審閱商譽的減值測試所用預
 值時對商譽進行減值測試。於 2023 年 12 月 31 日，管理層採用未來貼                                                   測的編製基準。我們亦讓內部估值專家協助
 現現金流模式對該等資產進行減值測試。由於評估較為複雜，涉及                                                             我們審閱商譽減值評估所採用的方法及若干
 諸如長期增長率及貼現率的重要判斷及假設，故此被視為關鍵審計                                                             假設。我們亦審閱管理層對貴集團減值模型
 事項。                                                                                       對合理可能變動的敏感性的評估並考量綜合
                                                                                           財務報表內減值測試披露的充足性。

2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION (CONTINUED)                                               2.1 編製基準（續）
    Basis of consolidation                                                           合併基準
    The consolidated financial statements include the financial statements          綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司
    of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the            （統 稱 為「本 集 團」）於 截 至 2023 年 12 月
    “Group”) for the year ended 31 December 2023. A subsidiary is an entity         31 日止年度之財務報表。附屬公司乃本
    (including a structured entity), directly or indirectly, controlled by the      公司直接或間接控制的實體（包括結構
    Company. Control is achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights,          性 實 體）。當 本 集 團 透 過 參 與 被 投 資 方
    to variable returns from its involvement with the investee and has the          而享有或有權獲得可變回報，且有能力
    ability to affect those returns through its power over the investee (i.e.,      透過對被投資方的權力影響該等回報（即
    existing rights that give the Group the current ability to direct the           現有權利賦予本集團目前指示被投資方
    relevant activities of the investee).                                           相關活動的能力）時，即屬於擁有控制權。

    Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting rights results       於一般情況下均存在多數投票權形成控
    in control. When the Company has less than a majority of the voting or           制權之推定。當本公司擁有的被投資方
    similar rights of an investee, the Group considers all relevant facts and        投票權或類似權利不及大半，則評估是
    circumstances in assessing whether it has power over an investee,                否擁有對被投資方的權力時，本集團會
    including:                                                                       考慮所有相關事實及情況，包括：

    (a)   the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders of the             (a)   與被投資方的其他投票持有者之
          investee;                                                                        間的合同安排；

    (b)   rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and                    (b)   從其他合同安排取得的權力；及

    (c)   the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.                     (c)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

    The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared for the same           附屬公司財務報表乃按與本公司一致的
    reporting period as the Company, using consistent accounting policies.           報告期及會計政策編製。附屬公司業績
    The results of subsidiaries are consolidated from the date on which the          的合併始於本集團獲得對該等附屬公司
    Group obtains control, and continue to be consolidated until the date            的控制權之時，並止於本集團喪失有關
    that such control ceases.                                                        控制權之時。

    Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive income are              損益和其他全面收入的各個組成部分歸
    attributed to the owners of the parent of the Group and to the non-              屬於本集團母公司的擁有人及非控股權
    controlling interests, even if this results in the non-controlling interests     益，即使此舉導致非控股權益有虧絀結
    having a deficit balance. All intra-group assets and liabilities, equity,        餘。所有集團內公司間的資產與負債、
    income, expenses and cash flows relating to transactions between                 權益、收入、開支及與本集團成員公司
    members of the Group are eliminated in full on consolidation.                    之間交易有關的現金流量均於綜合賬目

2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION (CONTINUED)                                                2.1 編製基準（續）
      Basis of consolidation (Continued)                                              合併基準（續）
      The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if facts and        倘有事實及情況顯示上文所述三項控制
      circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or more of the             因素中的一項或多項出現變化，本集團
      three elements of control described above. A change in the ownership            會重新評估其是否控制被投資方。並無
      interest of a subsidiary, without a loss of control, is accounted for as an     失去控制權的附屬公司擁有權權益變動
      equity transaction.                                                             以股本交易入賬。

      If the Group loses control over a subsidiary, it derecognises the related       倘本集團失去對附屬公司的控制權，則
      assets (including goodwill), liabilities, any non-controlling interest and      終止確認相關資產（包括商譽）、負債、
      the exchange fluctuation reserve; and recognises the fair value of any          任何非控股權益及匯兌波動儲備；並確
      investment retained and any resulting surplus or deficit in profit or loss.     認所保留任何投資的公允值及損益賬中

      currency is exchangeable into another currency and how it shall estimate       如何評估一種貨幣是否可兌換為另一種
      a spot exchange rate at a measurement date when exchangeability is             貨幣，以及當缺乏可兌換性時應如何估
      lacking. The amendments require disclosures of information that enable         計計量日的即期匯率。該等修訂要求披
      users of financial statements to understand the impact of a currency not       露資料令財務報表使用者能夠了解貨幣
      being exchangeable. Earlier application is permitted. When applying the        不可兌換的影響。允許提前應用。應用
      amendments, an entity cannot restate comparative information. Any              該等修訂時，實體不可重述比較資料。
      cumulative effect of initially applying the amendments shall be                首次應用該等修訂的任何累計影響應於
      recognised as an adjustment to the opening balance of retained profits         首次應該用日確認為對期初保留盈利的
      or to the cumulative amount of translation differences accumulated in a        調整，或於適當情況下對權益單獨組成
      separate component of equity, where appropriate, at the date of initial        部分中累計換算差額的累計金額的調
      application. The amendments are not expected to have any significant           整。預計該等修訂不會對本集團的財務
      impact on the Group’s financial statements.                                    報表產生任何重大影響。

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                                   2.4 重大會計政策
      Business combinations and goodwill                                             業務合併及商譽
      Business combinations are accounted for using the acquisition method.          業務合併乃以收購法列賬。已轉讓對價
      The consideration transferred is measured at the acquisition date fair         乃以收購日期的公允值計量，該公允值
      value which is the sum of the acquisition date fair values of assets           為本集團轉讓的資產於收購日期的公允
      transferred by the Group, liabilities assumed by the Group to the former       值、本集團向被收購方前擁有人承擔的
      owners of the acquiree and the equity interests issued by the Group in         負債，及本集團發行以換取被收購方控
      exchange for control of the acquiree. For each business combination,           制權的股本權益的總和。於各業務合併
      the Group elects whether to measure the non-controlling interests in           中，本集團選擇是否以公允值或被於收
      the acquiree that are present ownership interests and entitle their            購方可識別淨資產的應佔比例，計量於
      holders to a proportionate share of net assets in the event of liquidation     被收購方的非控股權益，即賦予持有人
      at fair value or at the proportionate share of the acquiree’s identifiable     在清盤時按比例分佔淨資產的現有所有
      net assets. All other components of non-controlling interests are              權權益。非控股權益的所有其他組成部
      measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs are expensed as              分均按公允值計量。收購相關成本於產
      incurred.                                                                      生時列為開支。

      The Group determines that it has acquired a business when the acquired         當所收購的一組活動及資產包括一項投
      set of activities and assets includes an input and a substantive process       入及一個實質性過程而兩者對創造產出
      that together significantly contribute to the ability to create outputs.       之能力有重大貢獻時，本集團確定其已
                                                                                     收購一項業務。

      When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the financial assets and       當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合同
      liabilities assumed for appropriate classification and designation in          條款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相關條
      accordance with the contractual terms, economic circumstances and              件，評估須承擔的金融資產及負債，以
      pertinent conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the             作出適合的分類及標示，其中包括分離
      separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of the acquiree.          被收購方主合同中的嵌入式衍生工具。

      If the business combination is achieved in stages, the previously held         如業務合併分階段進行，先前持有的股
      equity interest is remeasured at its acquisition date fair value and any       本權益按其於收購日期的公允值重新計
      resulting gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss.                        量，所產生的任何收益或虧損在損益中
                                                                                     確認。

    Goodwill is initially measured at cost, being the excess of the aggregate          商譽最初按成本計量，即已轉讓對價、
    of the consideration transferred, the amount recognised for non-                   非控股權益的確認金額及本集團先前持

                                                                                       收益。

    After initial recognition, goodwill is measured at cost less any                   於初始確認後，商譽按成本減任何累計
    accumulated impairment losses. Goodwill is tested for impairment                   減 值 虧 損 計 量。商 譽 須 每 年 作 減 值 測
    annually or more frequently if events or changes in circumstances                  試，若有事件發生或情況改變顯示賬面
    indicate that the carrying value may be impaired. The Group performs its           值有可能減值時，則會更頻密地進行測
    annual impairment test of goodwill as at 31 December. For the purpose              試。本集團於 12 月 31 日進行商譽的年度
    of impairment testing, goodwill acquired in a business combination is,             減值測試。為進行減值測試，因業務合
    from the acquisition date, allocated to each of the Group’s cash-                  併而購入的商譽自收購日期起被分配至
    generating units, or groups of cash-generating units, that are expected            預期可從合併產生的協同效益中獲益的
    to benefit from the synergies of the combination, irrespective of whether          本集團各現金產生單位或現金產生單位
    other assets or liabilities of the Group are assigned to those units or            組別，而無論本集團其他資產或負債是
    groups of units.                                                                   否已分配至該等單位或單位組別。

    Impairment is determined by assessing the recoverable amount of the                減值乃通過評估與商譽有關的現金產生
    cash-generating unit (group of cash-generating units) to which the                 單位（或現金產生單位組別）的可收回金
    goodwill relates. Where the recoverable amount of the cash-generating              額 釐 定。當 現 金 產 生 單 位（或 現 金 產 生
    unit (group of cash-generating units) is less than the carrying amount, an         單位組別）的可收回金額低於賬面金額
    impairment loss is recognised. An impairment loss recognised for                   時，確認減值虧損。已就商譽確認的減
    goodwill is not reversed in a subsequent period.                                   值虧損不得於隨後期間撥回。

    Where goodwill has been allocated to a cash-generating unit (or group              如商譽分配至現金產生單位（或現金產
    of cash-generating units) and part of the operation within that unit is            生單位組別）而該單位的部分業務已出

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (CONTINUED)                                             2.4 重大會計政策概要（續）
      Fair value measurement                                                               公允值計量
      Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to           公允值指於計量日期之市場參與者之間
      transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants           的有序交易中，就出售資產所收取之價
      at the measurement date. The fair value measurement is based on the                  格或轉讓債務所支付之價格。公允值計
      presumption that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability         量乃基於假設出售資產或轉讓債務之交
      takes place either in the principal market for the asset or liability, or in the     易於資產或負債之主要市場，或在未有
      absence of a principal market, in the most advantageous market for the               主要市場之情況下，則於資產或負債之
      asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market must be            最有利市場進行。主要或最有利市場須
      accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability is                位於本集團能到達之地方。資產或負債
      measured using the assumptions that market participants would use                    之公允值乃使用市場參與者為資產或負
      when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market participants act           債定價所用之假設計量（假設市場參與
      in their economic best interest.                                                     者依照彼等之最佳經濟利益行事）。

      A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account a               非金融資產之公允值計量乃經計及一名
      market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by using the              市場參與者透過使用其資產之最高及最
      asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to another market                 佳用途或透過將資產出售予將使用其最
      participant that would use the asset in its highest and best use.                    高及最佳用途之另一名市場參與者而能
                                                                                           夠產生經濟利益的能力。

      The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in the                      本集團使用適用於不同情況之估值技
      circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to measure fair            術，而其有足夠數據計量公允值，以盡
      value, maximising the use of relevant observable inputs and minimising               量利用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減少
      the use of unobservable inputs.                                                      使用不可觀察輸入數據。

      All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or disclosed in          於財務報表計量或披露公允值之所有資
      the financial statements are categorised within the fair value hierarchy,            產及負債，均根據對公允值計量整體而
      described as follows, based on the lowest level input that is significant to         言屬重要之最低層輸入數據在下述公允
      the fair value measurement as a whole:                                               值等級架構內進行分類：

      Level 1      —     based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets             第一層 —   按同等資產或負債於活躍
                         for identical assets or liabilities                                       市場之報價（未經調整）計
                                                                                                   算

      Level 2      —     based on valuation techniques for which the lowest                第二層 —   按 估 值 技 術 計 算（藉 此 直
                         level input that is significant to the fair value                         接或間接可觀察且對公允
                         measurement is observable, either directly or indirectly                  值計量而言屬重要之最低
                                                                                                   層輸入數據）
      Level 3      —     based on valuation techniques for which the lowest
                         level input that is significant to the fair value                 第三層 —   按 估 值 技 術 計 算（藉 此 不
                         measurement is unobservable                                               可觀察且對公允值計量而
                                                                                                   言屬重要之最低層輸入數
                                                                                                   據）

      For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the financial statements           就按經常性基準於財務報表確認之資產
      on a recurring basis, the Group determines whether transfers have                    及負債而言，本集團於各報告期末通過
      occurred between levels in the hierarchy by reassessing categorisation               重新評估分類（基於對公允值計量整體
      (based on the lowest level input that is significant to the fair value               而言屬重大之最低層輸入數據）以決定
      measurement as a whole) at the end of each reporting period.                         等級架構內各層之間是否有轉移。

    stated at cost less accumulated depreciation and any impairment losses.             本減累計折舊及任何減值虧損列賬。倘
    When an item of property, plant and equipment is classified as held for             物 業、廠 房 及 設 備 項 目 分 類 為 持 作 出
    sale or when it is part of a disposal group classified as held for sale, it is      售，或該項目屬於分類為持作出售之出
    not depreciated and is accounted for in accordance with HKFRS 5. The                售組別一部分，則不會計提折舊，並按
    cost of an item of property, plant and equipment comprises its purchase             香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 5 號 處 理 入 賬。物
    price and any directly attributable costs of bringing the asset to its              業、廠房及設備項目之成本包括其買價
    working condition and location for its intended use.                                及令該項資產達至其運作狀態及地點以
                                                                                        供擬定用途之任何直接應佔成本。

    Expenditure incurred after items of property, plant and equipment have              在物業、廠房及設備項目投入運作後所
    been put into operation, such as repairs and maintenance, is normally               產生之開支（例如維修及保養），一般於
    charged to profit or loss in the period in which it is incurred. In situations      產 生 期 間 計 入 損 益。倘 能 符 合 確 認 條
    where the recognition criteria are satisfied, the expenditure for a major           件，重大檢查開支可於資產賬面值資本
    inspection is capitalised in the carrying amount of the asset as a                  化以作代替。倘物業、廠房及設備之主
    replacement. Where significant parts of property, plant and equipment               要部分需定期更換，本集團會按特定使
    are required to be replaced at intervals, the Group recognises such parts           用年期確認該部份為個別資產，並據此
    as individual assets with specific useful lives and depreciates them                作出折舊。
    accordingly.

    Valuations are performed frequently enough to ensure that the fair value            估值會頻密進行，頻率足以確保重估資
    of a revalued asset does not differ materially from its carrying amount.            產的公允值不會大幅偏離其賬面值。物
    Changes in the values of property, plant and equipment are dealt with               業、廠房及設備的價值變動乃作為資產
    as movements in the asset revaluation reserve. If the total of this reserve         重估儲備的變動予以處理。倘按個別資
    is insufficient to cover a deficit, on an individual asset basis, the excess of     產基準計算，該儲備總額不足以彌補虧
    the deficit is charged to the statement of profit or loss and other                 絀，該虧絀的超額部分會自損益及其他
    comprehensive income. Any subsequent revaluation surplus is credited                全面收益表內扣除。任何其後重估盈餘
    to the statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income to                均會計入損益及其他全面收益表，惟以
    the extent of the deficit previously charged. An annual transfer from the           先前扣除的虧絀數額為限。每年由資產
    asset revaluation reserve to retained profits is made for the difference            重估儲備轉撥至保留利潤乃就根據資產
    between the depreciation based on the revalued carrying amount of an                重估賬面值的折舊與根據資產初始成本
    asset and the depreciation based on the asset’s original cost. On disposal          的折舊差額作出。於出售一項重估資產
    of a revalued asset, the relevant portion of the asset revaluation reserve          時，就先前估值變現的資產重估儲備的
    realised in respect of previous valuations is transferred to retained profits       相關部分會作為一項儲備變動轉撥至保
    as a movement in reserves.                                                          留利潤。

      Financial assets classified and measured at amortised cost are held               有。按攤銷成本分類及計量的金融資產
      within a business model with the objective to hold financial assets in            於持有金融資產的目的為收取合約現金

    Impairment of financial assets                                                     金融資產減值
    The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses (“ECLs”)              本集團確認對並非按公允值計入損益的
    for all debt instruments not held at fair value through profit or loss. ECLs       所有債務工具預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸
    are based on the difference between the contractual cash flows due in              虧損」）的撥備。預期信貸虧損乃基於根

    expects to receive, discounted at an approximation of the original                 期收取的所有現金流量之間的差額而釐
    effective interest rate. The expected cash flows will include cash flows           定、以原實際利率的近似值貼現。預期
    from the sale of collateral held or other credit enhancements that are             現金流量將包括出售所持抵押的現金流
    integral to the contractual terms.                                                 量或組成合約條款的其他信貸提升措施。

    General approach                                                                   一般方法
    ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for which there            預期信貸虧損分兩個階段進行確認。就
    has not been a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,      自初始確認起信貸風險並無大幅增加的
    ECLs are provided for credit losses that result from default events that           信貸敞口而言，會就未來 12 個月內可能
    are possible within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those                 發生違約事件而導致的信貸虧損（12 個
    credit exposures for which there has been a significant increase in credit         月預期信貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損。
    risk since initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit losses     就自初始確認起信貸風險大幅增加的該
    expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the              等信貸敞口而言，不論何時發生違約，
    timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).                                            於敞口的餘下年期內的預期信貸虧損均
                                                                                       須計提虧損撥備（全期預期信貸虧損）。

      At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the credit risk on a         於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具信
      financial instrument has increased significantly since initial recognition.     貸風險是否自初始確認起大幅增加。於
      When making the assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default            進行評估時，本集團比較金融工具於報
      occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting date with the         告日期發生違約的風險及金融工具於初
      risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of       始確認日期發生違約的風險，認為在毋
      initial recognition and considers reasonable and supportable information        須付出過多成本或努力的情況下即可獲
      that is available without undue cost or effort, including historical and        得合理可靠的資料（包括歷史及前瞻性
      forward-looking information. The Group considers that there has been a          資 料）。本 集 團 認 為 當 合 約 付 款 逾 期 30
      significant increase in credit risk when contractual payments are more          天以上時，信貸風險會大幅增加。
      than 30 days past due.

      The Group considers a financial asset in default when contractual               本集團會在合約付款逾期 90 日時考慮金
      payments are 90 days past due. However, in certain cases, the Group             融資產違約。然而，在若干情況下，當
      may also consider a financial asset to be in default when internal or           內部或外部資料顯示，在並無計及本集
      external information indicates that the Group is unlikely to receive the        團持有的任何信貸提升措施情況時，本
      outstanding contractual amounts in full before taking into account any          集團不大可能悉數收到未償還合約款
      credit enhancements held by the Group. A financial asset is written off         項，則本集團亦可認為金融資產違約。
      when there is no reasonable expectation of recovering the contractual           倘無法合理預期收回合約現金流量，則
      cash flows.                                                                     撇銷金融資產。

      Simplified approach                                                             簡化方法
      For trade receivables and contract assets that do not contain a significant     就並無重大融資成分的貿易應收款項及
      financing component or when the Group applies the practical expedient           合約資產或本集團未就重大融資成分的
      of not adjusting the effect of a significant financing component, the           影響作出調整的可行權宜方法而言，本
      Group applies the simplified approach in calculating ECLs. Under the            集團採用簡化法計算預期信貸虧損。根
      simplified approach, the Group does not track changes in credit risk, but       據簡化法，本集團並未追蹤信貸風險變
      instead recognises a loss allowance based on lifetime ECLs at each              動，轉而於各報告日期根據全期預期信
      reporting date. The Group has established a provision matrix that is            貸虧損確認虧損撥備。本集團已根據其
      based on its historical credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-           歷史信貸虧損經驗建立撥備矩陣，並就
      looking factors specific to the debtors and the economic environment.           債務人及經濟環境特定的前瞻性因素作
                                                                                      出調整。

    liabilities at fair value through profit or loss, loans and borrowings,               計入損益之金融負債、貸款及借款、應
    payables, or as derivatives designated as hedging instruments in an                   付款項或分類為指定作有效對沖的對沖
    effective hedge, as appropriate.                                                      工具的衍生工具（倘適用）。

    All financial liabilities are recognised initially at fair value and, in the case     所有金融負債初步按公允值確認，倘為
    of loans and borrowings and payables, net of directly attributable                    貸款及借貸以及應付款項，則須扣除直
    transaction costs.                                                                    接應佔交易成本。

    The Group’s financial liabilities include trade and other payables, lease             本集團的金融負債包括貿易及其他應付
    liabilities and interest-bearing bank borrowings.                                     款項、租賃負債以及計息銀行借款。

    Financial liabilities at amortised cost (trade and other payables,                    按攤銷成本列賬的金融負債（貿易及其
    and borrowings)                                                                       他應付款項以及借款）
    After initial recognition, trade and other payables, and interest-bearing             於首次確認後，貿易及其他應付款項以
    borrowings are subsequently measured at amortised cost, using the                     及計息貸款及借款隨後以實際利率法按
    effective interest rate method unless the effect of discounting would be              攤 銷 成 本 計 量，除 非 貼 現 影 響 微 不 足
    immaterial, in which case they are stated at cost. Gains and losses are               道，在該情況下則按成本列賬。當負債
    recognised in the statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive                 終止確認及按實際利率進行攤銷程序
    income when the liabilities are derecognised as well as through the                   時，其收益及虧損於損益及其他全面收
    effective interest rate amortisation process.                                         益表內確認。

    Amortised cost is calculated by taking into account any discount or                   攤銷成本於計算時已考慮收購事項任何
    premium on acquisition and fees or costs that are an integral part of the             折讓或溢價及屬實際利率不可或缺一部
    effective interest rate. The effective interest rate amortisation is included         分的費用或成本。實際利率攤銷計入損
    in finance costs in the statement of profit or loss and other                         益及其他全面收益表的融資成本內。
    comprehensive income.

    Derecognition of financial liabilities                                                終止確認金融負債
    A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under the                   當負債項下的責任被解除或取消或到期
    liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.                                     時，則終止確認金融負債。

    When an existing financial liability is replaced by another from the same             倘現有金融負債由同一貸方按條款迥異
    lender on substantially different terms, or the terms of an existing liability        的其他負債取代，或現有金融負債的條
    are substantially modified, such an exchange or modification is treated               款經重大修訂，則該等變更或修訂被視
    as a derecognition of the original liability and a recognition of a new               作終止確認原有負債並確認新負債，各
    liability, and the difference between the respective carrying amounts is              賬面值的差額於損益及其他全面收益表
    recognised in the statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive                 確認。
    income.

                                                                                       成本釐定。

      of changes in value and held for the purpose of meeting short-term cash          風險很小及為滿足短期現金承擔而持有。

      Provisions                                                                       撥備

      obligation.

    estimated future cash receipts over the expected life of the financial           或較短期間（如適用）內的估計未來現金
    instrument or a shorter period, when appropriate, to the net carrying            收入準確貼現至金融資產之賬面淨值的
    amount of the financial asset.                                                   比率予以確認。

    Dividend income is recognised when the shareholders’ right to receive            股息收入於股東收取款項之權利被確立
    payment has been established, it is probable that the economic benefits          時確認，與股息相關的經濟利益很可能
    associated with the dividend will flow to the Group and the amount of            流入本集團，且股息的金額能夠可靠計
    the dividend can be measured reliably.                                           量。

    Rental income is recognised on a time proportion basis over the lease            租金收入於租期內按時間比例基準確
    terms. Variable lease payments that do not depend on an index or a rate          認。並非視乎指數或利率而定的可變租
    are recognised as income in the accounting period in which they are              賃付款於其產生會計期間確認為收入。
    incurred.

    Contract liabilities                                                             合約負債
    A contract liability is recognised when a payment is received or a               倘本集團在轉讓相關貨品或服務前自客
    payment is due (whichever is earlier) from a customer before the Group           戶 收 取 付 款 或 付 款 到 期（以 較 早 者 為
    transfers the related goods or services. Contract liabilities are recognised     準），則 確 認 合 約 負 債。當 本 集 團 履 行
    as revenue when the Group performs under the contract (i.e., transfers           合約（即將相關貨品或服務控制權轉讓
    control of the related goods or services to the customer).                       予客戶）時，合約負債乃確認為收益。

    Right-of-return assets                                                           退貨權資產
    A right-of-return asset is recognised for the right to recover the goods         就有權收回預期客戶將退還的貨品確認
    expected to be returned by customers. The asset is measured at the               退貨權資產。資產按將退還貨品原賬面
    former carrying amount of the goods to be returned, less any expected            值減預期收回貨品的任何成本與退還貨
    costs to recover the goods and any potential decreases in the value of           品價值的任何潛在減幅計量。本集團就
    the returned goods. The Group updates the measurement of the asset               修訂預期退貨水平及退還貨品價值的任
    for any revisions to the expected level of returns and any additional            何額外減幅更新資產的計量。
    decreases in the value of the returned goods.

    Refund liabilities                                                               退款負債
    A refund liability is recognised for the obligation to refund some or all of     就退還自客戶收取（或應收）之部分或全
    the consideration received (or receivable) from a customer and is                部代價的義務確認退款負債，並按本集
    measured at the amount the Group ultimately expects it will have to              團最終預期其須退回予客戶的金額計
    return to the customer. The Group updates its estimates of refund                量。本集團於各報告期末更新其對退款
    liabilities (and the corresponding change in the transaction price) at the       負債之估計（及交易價格之相應變動）。
    end of each reporting period.

      customer type and rating, and coverage by letters of credit or other            級以及信用證或其他信貸保險形式的保
      forms of credit insurance). The calculation reflects the probability-           障 範 圍）的 逾 期 日 數 釐 定。該 計 算 反 映
      weighted outcome, the time value of money and reasonable and                    概率加權結果、貨幣時值及於報告日期
      supportable information that is available at the reporting date about           可得的有關過往事項、當前狀況及未來
      past events, current conditions and forecasts of future economic                經濟條件預測的合理及可靠資料。一般

27. RESERVES                                                                        27. 儲備
      The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements therein for the           本集團於本年度及過往年度的儲備金額
      current and prior years are presented in the consolidated statement of          及其變動於財務報表第 139 至 140 頁的綜
      changes in equity on pages 139 to 140 of the financial statements.              合權益變動表呈報。

      (i)    Statutory surplus reserve                                                (i)    法定盈餘儲備
             In accordance with the PRC regulations, certain companies in the                根 據 中 國 法 規，中 國 境 內 若 干 公
             PRC are required to transfer part of their profits after tax                    司 須 在 作 出 利 潤 分 派 前，將 其 部
             determined under the PRC accounting standards to the statutory                  分根據中國會計準則釐定的除稅
             surplus reserve fund, before profit distributions are made. The                 後 利 潤 轉 撥 至 法 定 盈 餘 儲 備 金。
             statutory surplus reserve fund is non-distributable and may be                  法 定 盈 餘 儲 備 金 不 可 分 派，可 用
             used either to offset losses, or for capitalisation issues by way of            於抵銷虧損或以實繳股本形式作
             paid-up capital.                                                                出資本化發行。

      (ii)   Capital reserve                                                          (ii)   資本儲備
             The amounts represented the balance of the credit amount arising                相關金額指實繳股本供款超出股
             from the excess of the par value of the shares from the paid-in                 份面值的進賬金額結餘。
             capital contribution.

      (iii) Other reserve                                                             (iii) 其他儲備
             This resulted from the acquisition of non-controlling shareholder.              因收購非控股股東而產生。

      (iv) Asset revaluation reserve                                                  (iv) 資產重估儲備

             owner-occupied property to the investment properties carried at                 物業變更用途為按公允值列賬之
             fair value.                                                                     投資物業而產生。

32. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                        32. 金融風險管理
    The Group does not have written risk management policies and                       本集團並無訂明書面風險管理政策及指
    guidelines. However, the board of directors meets periodically to analyse          引。然而，董事會定期舉行會議，分析
    and formulate measures to manage the Group’s exposure to market risk,              及制訂措施以管理本集團所面對的市場
    including credit risk, liquidity risk, foreign currency risk and interest rate     風險，包括信貸風險、流動資金風險、
    risk. As the directors of the Company consider that the Group’s exposure           外幣風險及利率風險。由於本公司董事
    to market risk is kept at a minimum level, the Group has not used any              認為本集團所面對的市場風險維持於最
    derivatives or other instruments for hedging purposes. The Group’s risk            低水平，本集團並無應用任何衍生或其
    management objectives and policies mainly focus on minimising the                  他工具以作對沖用途。本集團的風險管
    potential adverse effects of these risks on the Group by closely                   理目標及政策主要側重於透過密切監控
    monitoring the individual exposure as follows:                                     以下獨立風險，降低此等風險對本集團
                                                                                       的潛在不利影響：

    (a)   Credit risk                                                                  (a)   信貸風險
          The Group trades only with recognised and creditworthy third                       本集團僅與知名及信譽良好的第
          parties. It is the Group’s policy that all customers who wish to                   三 方 進 行 交 易。本 集 團 之 政 策 為
          trade on credit terms are subject to credit verification procedures.               所有有意按信貸條款進行買賣之
          In addition, receivable balances are monitored on an ongoing                       客 戶 均 須 接 受 信 貸 審 核 程 序。此
          basis and the Group’s exposure to bad debts is not significant. For                外，本 集 團 持 續 監 控 應 收 款 項 結
          transactions that are not denominated in the functional currency                   餘且本集團面臨的壞賬風險並不
          of the relevant operating unit, the Group does not offer credit                    重 大。對 於 並 非 以 相 關 營 運 單 位
          terms without the specific approval of the Head of Credit Control.                 的 功 能 貨 幣 計 值 的 交 易，未 經 信
                                                                                             貸 監 控 主 管 的 特 別 批 准，本 集 團
                                                                                             不會提供信貸條款。

          Maximum exposure and year-end staging                                              最大敞口及年末階段分類
          The tables below show the credit quality and the maximum                           下 表 顯 示 於 12 月 31 日 基 於 本 集 團
          exposure to credit risk based on the Group’s credit policy, which is               信 貸 政 策（主 要 基 於 過 往 既 有 資
          mainly based on past due information unless other information is                   料，除 非 有 毋 須 過 多 成 本 或 精 力
          available without undue cost or effort, and year-end staging                       即可獲取的其他資料可用）得出的
          classification as at 31 December.                                                  信貸質素及最大信貸風險敞口及

      (d)   Foreign currency risk                                                                (d)   外匯風險
            The Group has transactional currency exposures. Such exposures                             本集團承受交易帶來的外匯風險。
            arise from sales or purchases by operating units in currencies other                       相關風險因以經營單位功能貨幣
            than the units’ functional currencies. Approximately 61% (2022:                            以 外 的 幣 值 買 賣 而 產 生。本 集 團
            45%) of the Group’s sales were denominated in currencies other                             銷售額中約 61%（2022 年：45%）以
            than the functional currencies of the operating units making the                           作出銷售經營單位功能貨幣以外
            sale, whilst approximately 62% (2022: 80%) of costs were                                   的 幣 值 呈 列，而 約 62%（ 2022 年：
            denominated in the units’ functional currencies. The Group also                            80%）的成本以相關單位功能貨幣
            has certain bank balances denominated in NZD, USD and HKD.                                 呈 列。本 集 團 亦 擁 有 若 干 以 紐 西
                                                                                                       蘭元、美元及港元計值的銀行結餘。

      (f)   Capital management                                                        (f)   資本管理
            The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are to                 本集團資本管理之基本目標為保
            safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern and                障本集團之持續經營能力及維持
            to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its business             穩健資本比率支持業務發展並實
            and maximise shareholders’ value.                                               現股東價值最大化。

            The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments                   本 集 團 管 理 其 資 本 架 構，並 根 據
            to it in light of changes in economic conditions and the risk                   經濟環境變化及相關資產風險性
            characteristics of the underlying assets. To maintain or adjust the             質 作 出 調 整。為 維 持 或 調 整 資 本
            capital structure, the Group may adjust the dividend payment to                 架 構，本 集 團 可 能 調 整 向 股 東 派
            shareholders, return capital to shareholders or issue new shares.               息、向股東退還股本或發行新股。
            The Group is not subject to any externally imposed capital                      本集團不受任何外部施加的資本
            requirements. No changes were made in the objectives, policies or               要 求 規 限。截 至 2023 年 12 月 31 日
            processes for managing capital during the years ended 31                        及 2022 年 12 月 31 日 止 年 度，資 本
            December 2023 and 31 December 2022.                                             管理的目標、政策或程序概無變動。

            The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is net                  本集團使用資產負債比率（即債務
            debt divided by the adjusted capital plus net debt. The Group’s                 淨額除以經調整資本加債務淨額）
            policy is to maintain the gearing ratio between 10% and 30%. Net                監察資本。本集團的政策是將資產
            debt includes lease liabilities, trade payables, other payables and             負 債 比 率 維 持 介 乎 10% 至 30%。債
            accruals. The gearing ratios as at the end of the reporting periods             務淨額包括租賃負債、貿易應付款
